# QA Report — S304: Demo tier badges + gating copy

**Date:** 2026-08-28
**Branch:** `claude/demo-tier-badges-gating-1v74gr`
**QA author:** Claude Code
**Verdict:** ✅ PASS — five cards render four distinct tier badges with correct
tier↔tenant pairing on `/demos` and `/demos/admin`; all five homepage carousel
tabs show the matching plan; zero page errors; tsc / eslint / build all clean;
BL canary empty.

## How this was exercised

Real browser (Playwright + Chromium), `vite` dev server on `localhost:8080`,
viewport 1280×1400 @2x for the demo pages and 1440×900 @2x for the homepage.

**Two local-env accommodations, both disclosed:**

1. **Placeholder Supabase env.** `VITE_SUPABASE_URL` / `VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY`
   were set to placeholder values. Without them the app throws
   `supabaseUrl is required` at module init and renders nothing. Doppler is not
   reachable from this session.
2. **Seeded `window.__TENANT_BOOT__`.** `src/lib/subdomainRouter.ts:7` resolves
   the apex marketing site from `APEX_HOSTS = {pestflowpro.ai, www.pestflowpro.ai}`
   only, so on `localhost` `resolveTenantId()` returns null and
   `TenantBootProvider` renders its "Site Not Found" gate instead of the route.
   I pre-seeded the boot object — the same cache path `readCache()` uses in
   production to prevent a flash — so the router passes.

**Why this does not weaken the result:** both accommodations affect only tenant
*resolution*. The assertions below are about `DemoCard` / `ClientMockupCarousel`,
which render purely from the `DEMO_TENANTS` constant and `tierInfo()` with **no
network access whatsoever**. Nothing under test reads Supabase. Confirmed by
`page errors: none` on every run.

## Checks walked

| # | Check | Expected | Actual | Result |
|---|-------|----------|--------|--------|
| 1 | `/demos` card count | 5 | 5 | ✅ |
| 2 | `/demos` distinct tier badges | 4 (Pro doubled) | `Growth·$249/mo`, `Pro·$349/mo`, `Starter·$149/mo`, `Elite·$499/mo` | ✅ |
| 3 | `/demos/admin` card count + badges | same 5 / same 4 | identical | ✅ |
| 4 | Tier↔tenant pairing matches the brief | per table | all 5 correct (below) | ✅ |
| 5 | Gating copy on every card | 1 line, plain English | present on all 5 | ✅ |
| 6 | Elite copy differs (nothing above it) | no "next plan up" | "our top plan, so everything is unlocked." | ✅ |
| 7 | `/demos` subhead states plan dimension | extended | "…and five different plans…on the plan shown on its card." | ✅ |
| 8 | `/demos/admin` subhead states plan dimension | extended | "…each one runs on a different plan…what each plan does and does not include." | ✅ |
| 9 | Homepage carousel shows plan per tab | 5 correct | all 5 correct (below) | ✅ |
| 10 | Carousel chrome bar not cramped | legible | url + `ELITE` + `DEMO` fit with margin at 480px | ✅ |
| 11 | Console / page errors | none | `page errors: none` on all 3 runs | ✅ |
| 12 | No tier literal in the diff | 0 | 0 — all via `tierInfo()` | ✅ |
| 13 | BL canary `src/shells` + `app` | empty | empty | ✅ |
| 14 | `tsc` / `eslint` / `build` | clean | 0 / 0 errors / PASS | ✅ |

### Check 4 — rendered tier↔tenant pairing (`/demos`, verbatim)

```
[Growth · $249/mo]  shell=clean friendly   Coastal Pest Co.
[Pro · $349/mo]     shell=bold local       Urban Strike Pest Defense
[Pro · $349/mo]     shell=modern pro       Apex Pest Protection
[Starter · $149/mo] shell=rustic rugged    Heartland Pest Co.
[Elite · $499/mo]   shell=metro pro        Metro Pest Concierge
distinct badges: ["Growth · $249/mo","Pro · $349/mo","Starter · $149/mo","Elite · $499/mo"]
```

Cross-checked against the brief's table: heartland 1/Starter/rustic-rugged,
coastal 2/Growth/clean-friendly, apex 3/Pro/modern-pro, urban-strike
3/Pro/bold-local, metro 4/Elite/metro-pro. **All five match, shell included.**

### Check 5/6 — gating copy as rendered

```
This demo runs on the Growth plan. Anything you find locked inside is what the next plan up adds.
This demo runs on the Pro plan. Anything you find locked inside is what the next plan up adds.
This demo runs on the Starter plan. Anything you find locked inside is what the next plan up adds.
This demo runs on the Elite plan — our top plan, so everything is unlocked.
```

No internal code names ("tier", "entitlement", "gate", shell names) in any
user-facing string.

### Check 9 — homepage carousel, each tab clicked

```
Coastal      -> GROWTH  | url=coastal-pest.pestflowpro.ai
Urban Strike -> PRO     | url=urban-strike.pestflowpro.ai
Apex         -> PRO     | url=apex-protect.pestflowpro.ai
Heartland    -> STARTER | url=heartland-pest.pestflowpro.ai
Metro        -> ELITE   | url=metro-pest-concierge.pestflowpro.ai
page errors: none
```

# Wave 1b — grep results, verbatim

Run with ripgrep-equivalent recursive grep against a fresh checkout of `main`
(not GitHub code search, per the brief — it returns false negatives).
Scope: `*.ts *.tsx *.sql *.mjs *.js`, excluding `node_modules/` and `docs/`.

## Conclusion first

**No frontend surface reads `settings.subscription`. Nothing to fix.**

A Starter demo **cannot** display "Elite / $499" anywhere in its own dashboard.
`src/components/admin/BillingTab.tsx:163,165` is the only place a plan name and
price render to a logged-in client, and it reads
`usePlan()` → `tenants.entitlement` → `tierInfo()`. Lines 22–24 of that file
carry the S262 comment stating it no longer reads `settings.subscription`.

Classification of every hit:

- **`prospects` table columns, not tenant settings** — the Ironwood CRM sales
  pipeline. Renders, but from a completely different data path that has nothing
  to do with a tenant's live plan: `ProspectList.tsx:189,191`,
  `types.ts:41-42`, `ReportsTab.tsx:54,56,67`, `ProspectDetail.Onboarding.tsx:36-37`.
- **Writes / provisioning payloads, never reads-for-display** —
  `ProspectDetail.Provisioning.tsx:9-13,274-275`, `ClientSetupPayment.tsx:105-106`,
  `provision-tenant/index.ts:549-553`, `ironwood-provision/index.ts:95`,
  `stripe-webhook/index.ts:88`.
- **Derived from `tierInfo()` already** — `requestUpgrade.ts:31-32` sends
  `plan_name`/`monthly_price` in the upgrade-notify payload, sourced from
  `tierInfo(targetTier)`, so it cannot go stale.
- **The one real read of the settings key** — `outscraper-reviews/index.ts:112`
  gates manual review refresh on `settings.subscription.tier`. Renders nothing;
  logged as **L1** in the review doc, not fixed (gating is out of scope).

Collect on the Sales Call`,items:[{type:`body`,text:`Run through this list on every call. If a client doesn't have something handy, flag it for the intake form.`},{type:`heading`,text:`Business Basics`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Business name (official, as on their license)`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Owner name`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Phone number — the one displayed on the live site`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Email — for lead notifications and login`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Full address`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Business hours`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Tagline — one sentence description`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Founded year`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Number of technicians`},{type:`heading`,text:`Credentials`},{type:`bullet`,text:`State pest control license number`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Any certifications (QualityPro, etc.)`},{type:`heading`,text:`Services & Areas`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Every city/town they serve — be specific, not "Dallas area"`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Every pest service they offer`},{type:`heading`,text:`Online Presence`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Facebook page URL`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Instagram handle (optional)`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Google Business profile URL`},{type:`heading`,text:`Deal Terms`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Setup fee amount agreed`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Subscription tier: Starter $149 / Growth $249 / Pro $349 / Elite $499`}]},"theme-palette":{title:`Theme & Palette Selection`,items:[{type:`heading`,text:`The Four Themes`},{type:`step`,text:`Modern Pro — Clean dark hero, bold type. Best for established companies wanting a premium look.`},{type:`step`,text:`Clean Friendly — Bright, light layouts, friendly fonts. Best for owner-operators and small family businesses.`},{type:`step`,text:`Bold Shield — Strong visuals, shield/badge motifs. Best for companies emphasizing guarantees and certifications.`},{type:`step`,text:`Nature Fresh — Earth tones, natural aesthetic. Best for eco-friendly or green pest control companies.`},{type:`heading`,text:`Palette Tips`},{type:`tip`,text:`Match their truck wrap or logo colors if possible.`},{type:`tip`,text:`When in doubt: Modern Pro + Forest & Cream. Works for almost everyone.`},{type:`body`,text:`Ask: Do you want corporate/professional OR warm/approachable?`},{type:`heading`,text:`CTA Button Text`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Get a Free Quote (default — works for most)`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Schedule a Free Inspection (if they push inspections)`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Call Us Now (if they prefer phone calls)`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Book Online Today (if they use online booking)`}]},"prospect-fields":{title:`Filling the Prospect Record`,items:[{type:`body`,text:`Every field maps directly to the live site. Blank fields = placeholder text on launch.`},{type:`heading`,text:`Critical Fields`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Business Name — exactly as it should appear on the site`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Phone — displayed in header, hero, and contact page`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Admin Email + Password — client's login credentials`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Theme and Palette — locks the site's look and feel`},{type:`heading`,text:`Integrations`},{type:`step`,text:`Google Maps Embed URL: maps.google.com → Share → Embed a map → copy src URL from the iframe`},{type:`step`,text:`Google Place ID: developers.google.com/maps → Places → Place ID Finder → search business → copy ID`},{type:`step`,text:`Facebook Page ID: Facebook page → About → Page Transparency → Page ID`},{type:`tip`,text:`Integrations can be added after provisioning if needed — they don't block Create Site.`}]},invoice:{title:`Plan & Payment Guide`,items:[{type:`heading`,text:`Pricing Reference`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Starter  — $149/mo  |  Setup: $500–$1,000`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Growth   — $249/mo  |  Setup: $1,000–$1,500`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Pro      — $349/mo  |  Setup: $2,000–$3,500`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Elite    — $499/mo  |  Setup: $4,000–$10,000`},{type:`tip`,text:`"Most clients are on Growth — it hits the sweet spot of features and value."`},{type:`tip`,text:`Setup fee covers the build cost. Position it as a one-time investment, not a recurring charge.`},{type:`heading`,text:`If They Push Back on Setup Fee`},{type:`bullet`,text:`"We can look at waiving or reducing it depending on your timeline."`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Reducing fee is better than dropping a tier — keeps recurring revenue up.`},{type:`heading`,text:`Generating the Invoice`},{type:`step`,text:`Select the tier and confirm the setup fee amount.`},{type:`step`,text:`Click Generate Invoice — a Stripe link is created and emailed to the client.`},{type:`step`,text:`Send the subscription link separately after invoice is paid.`},{type:`warn`,text:`Do not send the intake link until setup fee payment is confirmed.`},{type:`heading`,text:`Confirming Payment`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Prospect status updates to Paid automatically via Stripe webhook.`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Or verify directly at dashboard.stripe.com.`}]},intake:{title:`Sending the Intake Link`,items:[{type:`tip`,text:`Send within 24 hours of payment confirmation — client is most engaged now.`},{type:`step`,text:`Click Send Intake Link in the prospect record.`},{type:`step`,text:`Client receives a unique email link to their 4-step intake form.`},{type:`step`,text:`Follow up by text or phone to confirm they received it.`},{type:`heading`,text:`The 4 Intake Steps`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Step 1: Business basics (name, phone, address, hours, tagline)`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Step 2: Services and service areas`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Step 3: Online presence (Facebook, Instagram, Google)`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Step 4: Branding extras (logo upload, notes)`},{type:`heading`,text:`Importing Intake Data`},{type:`step`,text:`Once client completes the form, click Import from Intake.`},{type:`step`,text:`Review all fields — manually fill any gaps.`},{type:`step`,text:`Click Save to confirm.`}]},"pre-provision":{title:`Pre-Provisioning Checklist`,items:[{type:`body`,text:`All items below must be complete before clicking Create Site.`},{type:`warn`,text:`Once provisioned, the slug (subdomain) is locked in. Double-check the business name.`},{type:`heading`,text:`Required`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Business Name`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Owner Name`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Phone Number`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Email Address`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Business Address`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Business Hours`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Theme selected`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Palette selected`},{type:`bullet`,text:`CTA Button Text`},{type:`bullet`,text:`At least one service area`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Admin Email set`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Admin Password set`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Setup fee marked as Paid`},{type:`heading`,text:`Strongly Recommended`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Google Maps Embed URL`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Google Place ID`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Facebook Page ID`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Tagline, founded year, number of technicians`}]},provisioning:{title:`Creating the Site`,items:[{type:`body`,text:`Create Site builds the subdomain, loads all prospect data, activates the admin dashboard, CRM, blog, and SEO tools.`},{type:`heading`,text:`Steps`},{type:`step`,text:`Complete the pre-provisioning checklist.`},{type:`step`,text:`Click Create Site.`},{type:`step`,text:`Wait 30–60 seconds — do not click again or navigate away.`},{type:`step`,text:`Success message shows the client's new subdomain URL.`},{type:`heading`,text:`If It Fails`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Read the error — it usually says what's missing.`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Common causes: missing required field, slug conflict, admin email already in use.`},{type:`warn`,text:`If you see "Slug already exists" — adjust the business name or slug before retrying.`},{type:`heading`,text:`Verify After Provisioning`},{type:`step`,text:`Visit the subdomain — confirm business name, phone, and address are correct.`},{type:`step`,text:`Click 2–3 service pages to confirm content loaded.`},{type:`step`,text:`Check the Contact page for hours and address.`}]},"post-launch":{title:`Post-Launch Steps`,items:[{type:`heading`,text:`Subscription`},{type:`step`,text:`Click Send Subscription Link in the prospect record.`},{type:`step`,text:`Client subscribes via Stripe secure checkout.`},{type:`step`,text:`Confirm subscription active before sending credentials.`},{type:`warn`,text:`Do not send credentials until subscription is confirmed.`},{type:`heading`,text:`Credentials`},{type:`step`,text:`Click Send Credentials.`},{type:`step`,text:`Client receives admin email and temporary password.`},{type:`step`,text:`Walk them through their first login on the handoff call.`},{type:`heading`,text:`Reveal Call Order`},{type:`step`,text:`Home page hero — let it speak for itself.`},{type:`step`,text:`Scroll to services — show 2–3 service pages.`},{type:`step`,text:`About page — name, founded year, team size.`},{type:`step`,text:`Contact page — phone, address, hours.`},{type:`step`,text:`Admin dashboard tour.`},{type:`step`,text:`End: "This is all yours — let me show you how to log in."`},{type:`tip`,text:`Lead with mobile view — most of their leads will find them on mobile.`}]},faq:{title:`Common Client Questions`,items:[{type:`heading`,text:`Can I change my colors after launch?`},{type:`body`,text:`Yes — reach out and we can update the palette anytime. It applies site-wide immediately.`},{type:`heading`,text:`Can I use my own domain?`},{type:`body`,text:`Yes. Custom domain setup is available — connect them with Scott. The subdomain stays active during transition.`},{type:`heading`,text:`Can I add a service page for a pest not listed?`},{type:`body`,text:`Yes — contact us to scope it. Some additions are included in the tier, others may have a small one-time fee.`},{type:`heading`,text:`Can I add team members to my account?`},{type:`body`,text:`Not self-service yet — contact us with the name and email and we'll set up access.`},{type:`heading`,text:`How do I write a blog post?`},{type:`body`,text:`Admin dashboard → Blog → New Post → write, add photo, hit Publish. Goes live immediately.`},{type:`heading`,text:`How do I see my leads?`},{type:`body`,text:`Admin dashboard → CRM. Every quote request shows name, phone, and what they need. Mark as contacted, booked, or closed.`},{type:`heading`,text:`Will the site show up on Google?`},{type:`body`,text:`Yes — built for local SEO. Results take 4–8 weeks. More blog posts = faster ranking.`},{type:`heading`,text:`What if I want to cancel?`},{type:`body`,text:`No long-term contracts. Site stays active through end of billing period. Just reach out.`}]}};function Je(e,t,n){switch(e.type){case`heading`:return(0,g.jsx)(`p`,{className:`font-bold text-gray-800 mt-4 mb-1 Collect on the Sales Call`,items:[{type:`body`,text:`Run through this list on every call. If a client doesn't have something handy, flag it for the intake form.`},{type:`heading`,text:`Business Basics`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Business name (official, as on their license)`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Owner name`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Phone number — the one displayed on the live site`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Email — for lead notifications and login`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Full address`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Business hours`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Tagline — one sentence description`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Founded year`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Number of technicians`},{type:`heading`,text:`Credentials`},{type:`bullet`,text:`State pest control license number`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Any certifications (QualityPro, etc.)`},{type:`heading`,text:`Services & Areas`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Every city/town they serve — be specific, not "Dallas area"`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Every pest service they offer`},{type:`heading`,text:`Online Presence`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Facebook page URL`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Instagram handle (optional)`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Google Business profile URL`},{type:`heading`,text:`Deal Terms`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Setup fee amount agreed`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Subscription tier: Starter $149 / Growth $249 / Pro $349 / Elite $499`}]},"theme-palette":{title:`Theme & Palette Selection`,items:[{type:`heading`,text:`The Four Themes`},{type:`step`,text:`Modern Pro — Clean dark hero, bold type. Best for established companies wanting a premium look.`},{type:`step`,text:`Clean Friendly — Bright, light layouts, friendly fonts. Best for owner-operators and small family businesses.`},{type:`step`,text:`Bold Shield — Strong visuals, shield/badge motifs. Best for companies emphasizing guarantees and certifications.`},{type:`step`,text:`Nature Fresh — Earth tones, natural aesthetic. Best for eco-friendly or green pest control companies.`},{type:`heading`,text:`Palette Tips`},{type:`tip`,text:`Match their truck wrap or logo colors if possible.`},{type:`tip`,text:`When in doubt: Modern Pro + Forest & Cream. Works for almost everyone.`},{type:`body`,text:`Ask: Do you want corporate/professional OR warm/approachable?`},{type:`heading`,text:`CTA Button Text`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Get a Free Quote (default — works for most)`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Schedule a Free Inspection (if they push inspections)`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Call Us Now (if they prefer phone calls)`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Book Online Today (if they use online booking)`}]},"prospect-fields":{title:`Filling the Prospect Record`,items:[{type:`body`,text:`Every field maps directly to the live site. Blank fields = placeholder text on launch.`},{type:`heading`,text:`Critical Fields`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Business Name — exactly as it should appear on the site`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Phone — displayed in header, hero, and contact page`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Admin Email + Password — client's login credentials`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Theme and Palette — locks the site's look and feel`},{type:`heading`,text:`Integrations`},{type:`step`,text:`Google Maps Embed URL: maps.google.com → Share → Embed a map → copy src URL from the iframe`},{type:`step`,text:`Google Place ID: developers.google.com/maps → Places → Place ID Finder → search business → copy ID`},{type:`step`,text:`Facebook Page ID: Facebook page → About → Page Transparency → Page ID`},{type:`tip`,text:`Integrations can be added after provisioning if needed — they don't block Create Site.`}]},invoice:{title:`Plan & Payment Guide`,items:[{type:`heading`,text:`Pricing Reference`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Starter  — $149/mo  |  Setup: $500–$1,000`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Growth   — $249/mo  |  Setup: $1,000–$1,500`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Pro      — $349/mo  |  Setup: $2,000–$3,500`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Elite    — $499/mo  |  Setup: $4,000–$10,000`},{type:`tip`,text:`"Most clients are on Growth — it hits the sweet spot of features and value."`},{type:`tip`,text:`Setup fee covers the build cost. Position it as a one-time investment, not a recurring charge.`},{type:`heading`,text:`If They Push Back on Setup Fee`},{type:`bullet`,text:`"We can look at waiving or reducing it depending on your timeline."`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Reducing fee is better than dropping a tier — keeps recurring revenue up.`},{type:`heading`,text:`Generating the Invoice`},{type:`step`,text:`Select the tier and confirm the setup fee amount.`},{type:`step`,text:`Click Generate Invoice — a Stripe link is created and emailed to the client.`},{type:`step`,text:`Send the subscription link separately after invoice is paid.`},{type:`warn`,text:`Do not send the intake link until setup fee payment is confirmed.`},{type:`heading`,text:`Confirming Payment`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Prospect status updates to Paid automatically via Stripe webhook.`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Or verify directly at dashboard.stripe.com.`}]},intake:{title:`Sending the Intake Link`,items:[{type:`tip`,text:`Send within 24 hours of payment confirmation — client is most engaged now.`},{type:`step`,text:`Click Send Intake Link in the prospect record.`},{type:`step`,text:`Client receives a unique email link to their 4-step intake form.`},{type:`step`,text:`Follow up by text or phone to confirm they received it.`},{type:`heading`,text:`The 4 Intake Steps`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Step 1: Business basics (name, phone, address, hours, tagline)`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Step 2: Services and service areas`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Step 3: Online presence (Facebook, Instagram, Google)`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Step 4: Branding extras (logo upload, notes)`},{type:`heading`,text:`Importing Intake Data`},{type:`step`,text:`Once client completes the form, click Import from Intake.`},{type:`step`,text:`Review all fields — manually fill any gaps.`},{type:`step`,text:`Click Save to confirm.`}]},"pre-provision":{title:`Pre-Provisioning Checklist`,items:[{type:`body`,text:`All items below must be complete before clicking Create Site.`},{type:`warn`,text:`Once provisioned, the slug (subdomain) is locked in. Site.`},{type:`step`,text:`Wait 30–60 seconds — do not click again or navigate away.`},{type:`step`,text:`Success message shows the client's new subdomain URL.`},{type:`heading`,text:`If It Fails`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Read the error — it usually says what's missing.`},{type:`bullet`,text:`Common causes: missing required field, slug conflict, admin email already in use.`},{type:`warn`,text:`If you see "Slug already exists" — adjust the business name or slug before retrying.`},{type:`heading`,text:`Verify After Provisioning`},{type:`step`,text:`Visit the It applies site-wide immediately.`},{type:`heading`,text:`Can I use my own domain?`},{type:`body`,text:`Yes. Custom domain setup is available — connect them with Scott. The subdomain stays active during transition.`},{type:`heading`,text:`Can I add a service page for a pest not listed?`},{type:`body`,text:`Yes — contact us to scope it. Some additions are included in the tier, others may have a small one-time fee.`},{type:`heading`,text:`Can I add team members to my account?`},{type:`body`,text:`Not self-service yet — contact us with the name and email and we'll set up access.`},{type:`heading`,text:`How do I write a blog post?`},{type:`body`,text:`Admin dashboard → Blog → New Post → write, add photo, hit Publish. Goes live immediately.`},{type:`heading`,text:`How do I see my leads?`},{type:`body`,text:`Admin dashboard → CRM. Every quote request shows name, phone, and what they need. Mark as contacted, booked, or closed.`},{type:`heading`,text:`Will the site show up on Google?`},{type:`body`,text:`Yes — built for local SEO. Results take 4–8 weeks. Four consumers. The homepage carousel the brief could not locate is
**`src/pages/marketing/ClientMockupCarousel.tsx`**, rendered by
`src/pages/marketing/sections/MarketingHero.tsx:63`. `DemoCard.tsx` imports the
`DemoTenant` type and URL helpers, not the array.

**`shortLabel` has exactly one consumer** — `ClientMockupCarousel.tsx:7`, the tab
pill label. `DemoCard.tsx` does not read it. Its meaning is unchanged by this
session; `tier` was added as an independent field.
